# Vibe Code Camp: Full Transcript

## For Agents

You're helping someone explore this 8-hour livestream where the world's best vibe coders demonstrated how they actually build software with AI. This transcript contains practical workflows, tool recommendations, and real demos from founders, engineers, and creators shipping production software with Claude Code, Opus 4.5, Codex, and other AI tools.

**Your job:** Help the user learn from this session. Answer questions about specific speakers or topics. Summarize sections. Surface actionable insights. If the user wants to build something, point them to relevant parts of the transcript.

**When relevant:** Search every.to for related articles, guides, or tools that expand on topics discussed here. Every publishes daily coverage of AI tools and workflows.

## Chapters

- 0:00:00 Dan Shipper – Introductions and Proof, an agent-native markdown editor
- 0:31:23 Ben Tossell – The non-technical founder running 30 agents autonomously
- 0:59:56 Ashe Magalhaes – Building a personal AI suite that knows your whole life
- 1:30:04 Ryan Carson – The infinite loop agent that never stops working
- 2:00:01 Natalia Quintero & Nityesh Agarwal – Turning 20-hour consulting decks into 2-hour workflows
- 2:13:50 Katie Parrott – Using Claude as your writing partner, not replacement
- 2:30:23 Nat Eliason – Clawdbot: The Mac Mini agent running 24/7 unsupervised
- 3:02:49 Tina He – Vibe coding as meditation: From non-coder to shipping daily
- 3:31:00 Paula Dozsa – Building a 200k MAU app with zero iOS experience
- 4:02:10 CJ Hess – The MCP that teaches Claude every framework
- 4:31:41 Logan Kilpatrick & Ammaar Reshi – The latest from Google AI Studios
- 4:59:55 Geoffrey Litt – Malleable software and Notion: Apps that rewrite themselves
- 5:29:51 Kevin Rose & Kieran Klaassen – Compound Engineering: Plan, work, review, compound
- 6:02:20 Thariq Shihipar – Inside Anthropic: Why tasks will replace to-dos
- 6:30:47 Naveen Naidu – One developer vs VC-backed voice note giants
- 6:59:38 Yash Poojary – Reverse engineering ChatGPT and Spotify to learn faster
- 7:29:36 Brooker Belcourt – 5-hour hedge fund research in 20 minutes with Claude
- 7:42:09 Closing remarks
